## 328. Founder amendment — Home deduplication and Production balance mode (2026-09-28)

**Findings addressed:** §327 recorded that Production builds with `NEXT_PUBLIC_BALANCE_FROM_LEDGER=false` (wallet mode) while CI builds the ledger default, which let the T3/T5 defect reach Production until #732. It also recorded that default simulated Home showed the latest entry twice.

**Decision 1 — Production balance mode:** The Founder said “ledger is intended so we can work downstream from that”. Ledger mode, the documented default and source of truth in `lib/state/balance.mode.ts`, is Production's intended configuration. The Founder-owned action is to remove `NEXT_PUBLIC_BALANCE_FROM_LEDGER=false` from the Vercel Production environment (or set it to `true`) and redeploy. No repository change is required; the flag remains a documented rollback. After the change, the steward verifies with the §327 probe (ledger $5 and wallet $7 on Home shows $5) and re-runs the closeout inspection on Production. [Done 5 Oct 2026 (§341): probe $5.00; inspection 82/82.] Browsers whose stored wallet balance has no matching ledger entries will show the ledger figure after the switch. The research route is unreleased, so exposure is limited. A wallet-mode CI job is not adopted. The wallet-mode unit tests from #732 are retained. [Superseded §344, 5 Oct 2026: the flag stops being the documented rollback (rollback is a single revert of the runtime PR), and the #732 wallet-mode unit tests are removed with the wallet branch.]

**Decision 3 — Recent activity overlap (amendment, 2026-09-28):** After #738 merged, the Founder chose to also clean up the remaining overlap: in the several-changes case, Recent activity repeated the entries listed under “Since you were last here”. The Founder said “merge is done - we can perform the cleanup” and selected “Recent activity overlap” and “Hide when ‘since’ lists”. Under the same ticket and allowed files, default-route Recent activity is hidden while the one-change or several-changes variant shows, and kept in every other state. The §7a brief records the exact states. This source amendment and its own separate verified RAP rebind precede the runtime edit.

**Decision 2 — Home deduplication:** The Founder said “We need to clean that up lets go with our latest direction ‘Since you were last here’”. Finite nested Lane V ticket `CLASS-A-VAL-002-HOME-DEDUP-001` (brief in §7a) removes the default-route “Latest change” strip. It supersedes, for that strip only, the §7a HOME-EXPERIENCE-001 decision-4 retention of existing default Home elements; Recent activity and the empty states stay.

**Sequence:** The §327 closeout rebind completed in #736 (`72979e8`). This source record, then its own separate verified permanent-main RAP rebind, then the runtime PR with an independent Verifier PASS on its exact head, Production inspection, a source-first completion record and a final RAP rebind. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

## 341. Production ledger balance mode verified (§328 decision 1, 2026-10-05)

**Date:** 2026-10-05

**Founder action:** In the 5 Oct 2026 steward session, the Founder (Musalwa Hibajene) performed the §328 decision 1 action. The Founder changed `NEXT_PUBLIC_BALANCE_FROM_LEDGER` in the Vercel Production environment and redeployed, then reported “Production deployment has been done”. The Founder did not state whether the variable was removed or set to `true`; both satisfy §328. The shipped bundle reads the variable at runtime rather than inlining a value, which is consistent with removal.

**Founder approval to record:** Musalwa Hibajene, 5 Oct 2026 (AWST), in the same session: “spin up a opus 5.5 orchestrator who will action the docs only record. The orchestrator should spin up a gpt 6.1 agent for verification review of the PR once the commit is done”.

**Status:** docs-only record of a completed Founder-owned Production configuration action. No ticket is activated, closed or amended. This record does not change occupancy; live §7 / §7a remain the occupancy surface. The RAP permanent-main rebind for this source follows separately.

**Deployment:** GitHub Production deployment `6856469933` for commit `82c5d3bf6cc8483bbc820217b240e006f243fc76` (#764, docs-only) received a second success status at 2026-10-05T10:42:22Z (Vercel URL `hedgr-copilot-frontend-f81zbwg0q-hedgr.vercel.app`). This matches the Founder's redeploy; the frontend runtime is unchanged from the prior Production build. The later automatic Production build of `bed0c96` (#767) also reads the variable at runtime (alias bundle checked 5 Oct 2026).

**Verification (steward, public alias `https://hedgr-copilot-frontend.vercel.app`):**
- **Bundle, before:** the `/dashboard` client bundle folded `getBalanceMode` to `return "wallet"` with `"false"` inlined.
- **Bundle, after (~10:45 UTC):** the variable is no longer inlined; `getBalanceMode` evaluates at runtime and falls back to `ledger`.
- **§327 probe (ledger $5, wallet $7):** Home shows **$5.00** at 390 × 844 and 1440 × 1024 (previously $7.00; `HOME_EXPERIENCE_QA.md` and §329).
- **Closeout inspection re-run in ledger mode (10:48 UTC):** **82/82 checks passed** at 390 and 1440 px in fresh Chromium contexts, with mock sign-in and browser-local simulated entries only. No previous visit: Recent activity shown. One change: the one-sentence “One simulated withdrawal of $2.00 on 5 Oct took your position from $5.00 to $3.00.”, with Recent activity hidden. Several changes: the since list shown and Recent activity hidden. No change: “Nothing has changed since …” with Recent activity shown. Pending: ledger total $4.00 includes the pending deposit, and +$1.00 is listed. Journey Home control: $3.00, no Recent activity. Every case had no “Latest change” strip, no horizontal overflow and no page error.
- **Harness disclosure:** the first two runs scored 73/80 and 78/80. Every failure was a harness fault: a last-visit seed race after login; a wrong pending expectation (the ledger total includes pending); and expecting a list where the one-change state renders a sentence. After the harness was fixed, the run passed 82/82. Evidence is kept locally and not committed; `HOME_EXPERIENCE_QA.md` records the table and file hashes.

**Accepted side effect (§328):** a browser whose stored wallet balance lacks matching ledger entries now shows the ledger figure. The research route is unreleased, so exposure is limited. The flag remains the documented rollback. [Superseded §344, 5 Oct 2026: the flag is retired by `CLASS-A-VAL-002-STABILITY-LEDGER-ONLY-001`; rollback is a single revert of its runtime PR.] No wallet-mode CI job is adopted.

**Limitations:** The exact Vercel change (deleted or set to `true`) was not stated by the Founder. No Vercel CLI or dashboard read was used; deployment identity comes from GitHub deployment statuses.

**Effect:** none on doctrine, ADRs, `.cursorrules`, occupancy, sequencing or ticket scope. This record closes the §328 / §329 “ledger switch outstanding” note. It grants no participant release, financial capability or cross-lane authority. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

## 344. Founder activation — nested Lane V wallet balance mode retirement (`CLASS-A-VAL-002-STABILITY-LEDGER-ONLY-001`, 2026-10-05)

**Date:** 2026-10-05

**Founder direction:** Musalwa Hibajene, 5 Oct 2026 (AWST), in session: “lets also fix the wallet vs ledgr configuration. I would recommend retiring the wallet configuration from production paths.”

**Founder approval:** Musalwa Hibajene, 5 Oct 2026 (AWST), later in the same session: “approved to proceed”. This answers the steward proposal, which asked for approval of (a) the scope in the live §7a brief, (b) both §328 supersessions below, and (c) the full sequence: source-first activation → separate RAP rebind → runtime PR → exact-head independent Verifier → merge → Production probe → source-first closeout → separate RAP rebind.

**Status:** source-first nested Lane V activation under open parent `CLASS-A-VAL-002`. Class A, synthetic. No D-number. Source merge and a separate verified permanent-main RAP rebind precede any runtime.

**Foundation layer:** **Stability** under `docs/strategy/PHASE-SEQUENCING-2026-10-05.md`. Ledger becomes the only balance source, so the balance a user sees no longer depends on a build-time flag. This is not research polish and not Stability Engine spec work (which the Founder owns personally). It changes no Engine / `EngineState` and respects the dissemination hold.

**Why:** Production has run ledger mode since §341 (82/82). The wallet branch is now dead code, and the configuration split (Production wallet mode, CI ledger only) let the §327 T3/T5 defect reach Production until #732. Retiring the branch removes that drift class.

**Supersessions of §328 (Founder-approved):**

No new posture; existing `EnginePosture` may be read-only context or omitted, never given a second meaning. Storage and clock are not inputs; time (`asOf`) is explicit. Engineering determinism and capital stability remain separate meanings; §346's three-property test contract remains binding.

**Founder activation — Home small fixes (2026-10-09; §355):** Founder Musalwa Hibajene (repo owner `mhibajene`) approved activation of the Home small-fixes slice on 9 Oct 2026 at 13:42 AWST and, at 13:50 AWST, directed that it follow the one Home experience as fixes and approved proceeding. These are fixes within the one Home experience, not a route divergence; no journey-only gate is added, and the HOME-EXPERIENCE T3 change chip and T5 chip fade are superseded on both routes. Live-mode Home is unchanged. The Founder approved this Home work as fixes ahead of the Phase-Sequencing order; on this ticket's closeout, focus returns to Stability Engine work. No visualiser prototype, Planning-targets wording, display-rate or local-figure change, research release, Lane E/G or financial capability follows. Source merge and a separate independently verified permanent-main RAP rebind precede test-only red-first runtime under §346. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

**Founder instruction / answers:** Musalwa Hibajene, 9 Oct 2026 at 13:42 AWST: “Yes — activate the Home small-fixes slice (1–6) as relayed by HedgrOps”. Then at 13:50 AWST: “Let’s follow the one home experience esp considering that these are fixes. Approved to proceed and then focus returns to stability engine work”. Scope relayed by HedgrOps on 9 Oct 2026 from `KIP-ACTIVITY-VISUALISER-EXPLORATION-001` and confirmed by the Founder. Answers: D1 both Home routes as one Home experience (§327), with no journey-only prop, class or branch added (the pre-existing journey utilities branch from §327 is reused for D3); D2 the chip does not render while the What changed or Since card shows, which at `2888cb5` holds in every state where the chip rendered on either route, so the chip is retired from Home on both routes, superseding the HOME-EXPERIENCE T3 chip and T5 chip fade (hero count-up, line settle and arrival announcement remain); D3 second-slot swap on the journey utilities (deposit stays slot 1; slot 2 is How this simulation works → Simulate a withdrawal → View Activity by completed count 0/1/≥2), reusing the approved "Simulate a withdrawal"; default Home keeps its three utilities, which already offer the withdrawal at step 3; D4 both axis labels (date and "Today") removed; D5 neutral ink for amounts only; D6 Start-here step circles/markers unchanged; D7 ticket `CLASS-A-VAL-002-HOME-SMALL-FIXES-001`, failing tests first under §346, no waiver.

The HOME-EXPERIENCE copy register is otherwise unchanged; "display estimate" stays character-for-character and only its layout changes. Live-mode Home is unchanged.

**Basis / sequencing:** Home layer under `docs/strategy/PHASE-SEQUENCING-2026-10-05.md`, which places Home after Stability. ## 362. Founder activation — Home example picker (`CLASS-A-VAL-002-HOME-EXAMPLE-PICKER-001`, 2026-10-10)

**Founder activation — Home example picker (2026-10-10; §362):** Founder Musalwa Hibajene (repo owner `mhibajene`), in Claude Code, approved the Product Experience proposal and its decisions on 10 Oct 2026 and said "go, record the activation" at ~09:15 AWST. Activate only `CLASS-A-VAL-002-HOME-EXAMPLE-PICKER-001` as the sole nested Lane V ticket under open `CLASS-A-VAL-002` (Home layer, Class A informational/synthetic, Green classification only; no standing delegation). The controlling brief is in §7a. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

### Verbatim Founder instructions (10 Oct 2026, Claude Code)

> "we've just finished the Daniel slice on the synthetic journey. And I'm just thinking there could be a more um, efficient way of laying out the examples, probably like a drop down at the top in, in the synthetic journey um, that allows a user to be able to sort of experience what happens or be walked through that example. Uh, as opposed to having it statically, um, statically set in the what's changed uh, in the what's changed panel, just because uh, we'll also have other examples. So Sarah Slice is going to come after this, and it would just be more efficient to allow a user to experience the examples and those scenarios, but also be able to have a blank slate or a clean slate to be able to see what happens when they simulate their own journey."

> "Picker first, then walkthroughs; remove Daniel from default dashboard."

> "Sarahs feedback which I agree with:" (Sarah's review, supplied by the Founder: name each option after the person and their situation, "Sarah's course fee", "Daniel's reserve", "Start fresh"; dropdown label "Choose an example"; avoid "Example" and currency-tied names; agree with hiding the mock guidance in Daniel mode because showing guidance next to a "not connected" note invites "is this what Hedgr will show me?")

> Answer to "Start fresh" behaviour: "Keep their simulation (Recommended)"

> "Lets go with "Your own Simulation" which is more accurate"

> "go, record the activation"

### Decisions as put and accepted

- **Order:** picker before Sarah's slice, so Sarah lands as a picker entry rather than a second fixed Home panel. A Home ticket ahead of the next Stability ticket, following §355.
- **Depth:** picker only; authored walkthrough steps are a later ticket.
- **Default `/dashboard`:** Daniel removed.
- **Mock guidance in Daniel's reserve:** hidden; the coexistence line is removed with it.
- **Default option behaviour:** keeps the user's own simulation (no reset); clearing stays with `reset=1`.
- **Copy:** label `Choose an example`; options `Your own simulation` and `Daniel's reserve`; `Sarah's course fee` reserved. The Steward used sentence case for "Your own simulation", matching Home copy; the Founder's message capitalised "Simulation". No helper line, since the option name already states it.
- **Mockup:** an inline Product Experience mockup was reviewed before these decisions; it is non-authoritative evidence only.

**Predecessor / effect:** §361 is complete after source closeout #825 (`582ea779a25877b1f3dd437d5235d6cf992bf442`) and final verified RAP #826 (`7ca19e6ecd709bc7564b7746134c86d33431e93c`). §362 is the next free record; the ticket ID has no collision. This activation supplies the sole new nested Lane V authority. It supersedes §359 D1 and D2 for this ticket only (see §7a); §359 D3–D5, its rate decision and A/A1 remain in force, and historical records stay intact. No new D-number.

**Controlling brief / boundaries:** §7a names each runtime/test/source path, named red→green tests, exclusions, stop/rollback and all gates. No runtime in this source PR. Source merge and separate independently verified permanent-main RAP rebind precede effective activation. Steward stops there; implementation is launched separately. > "I want us to refine this. A drop down has been implemented to improve legibility. I do think for the Daniel slice (and subsequent examples) - it will help for the user to see the full home dashboard so that have an appreciation of what the scenario looks like experientially. Decision, ~13:12 AWST, answering the Product Experience Lead decision sheet:

> "1-6 as recommended, currency context side by side. For activation gpt 6.1 high, implementation use gpt 6.1 extra high agent"

### Decisions as put and accepted

1. **Ticket shape:** one Home ticket for the example-scenario full Home pattern plus Daniel's reserve. Sarah follows later as her own Stability slice and then her own Home entry; SME remains held. Authored walkthrough steps stay deferred. This records the pattern, not authority to build other examples.
2. **Timeline / explicit §359 amendment:** O1 rate 24.5 → O2 rate 29.5 (Daniel's last visit) → O3 rate 27 (now), producing Engine `localDisplay` K19,600 → K23,600 → K21,600. Three calls to existing `computeDanielRead` over a frozen runtime input list. O3 **is** the existing `DANIEL_HOME_FIXTURE_INPUT`, and its read equals unchanged `DANIEL_HOME_READ`. Accept the proposal's fixed fictional placeholder dates: O1 `2026-09-09T00:00:00.000Z`, O2 `2026-09-25T00:00:00.000Z`, O3 unchanged `2026-10-09T00:00:00.000Z`. This explicitly amends §359's rate decision and D4 **for example-history inputs only**: 24.5 and 29.5 are historical runtime fixture inputs, never alternate current rates. The current read, display table, synthetic normalization and `lib/fx.ts` are unchanged; 27 remains the only current simulated ZMW source. No clock, test-golden or research-fixture input.
3. **Hero / module set / explicit §362 amendment:** adopt proposal §2, with decision 7 below overriding only its hidden currency-context row. Daniel's hero uses the existing `Daniel’s declared holding · Fictional example` heading, verbatim current K21,600, `display estimate` below, and existing fictional USD 800 holding line. No “balance” (ADR 0027 decision 3) or relabelling Daniel's holding as a position. Reuse `PositionLine` with an optional label prop and default own-simulation label unchanged, existing styling/step shape, canonical Engine minor-unit points and no arrival motion. Adapt the change card to O2/O3 Engine strings. Keep orientation, picker and unchanged Important disclosures. Hide the date line, display-currency selector, deposit/withdraw/View Activity/restart controls, user's Recent activity, Start-here, planning targets, posture/notices/mock guidance and coexistence line. This explicitly supersedes **§362 D3's Daniel-mode render list for this ticket only**; D4 state isolation and D5 eligibility remain.
4. **Actions:** all hidden, plus one `Back to your own simulation` link, changing only the URL. It preserves the journey, removes `example` and drops `reset` as the existing picker does. It never resets or writes the simulation.
5. **Change-card wording / explicit §359 amendment:** Engine `explanation.holding` and `explanation.rateAssumption` verbatim for O2 and O3; no authored explanation or computed difference. This un-defers **§359 D3's holding string for this surface**. Reuse `This is what happened in the example. It doesn’t tell you what will happen next.` verbatim.
6. **asOf:** display the same fact as a short date, e.g. `9 Oct 2026`; unchanged underlying value and `<time dateTime>`. Card dates identify the fixed fictional O2/O3 observations, not the user's visits or today's clock. Existing `formatShortDate` yields day/month; year presentation may be local to an allowlisted presentation file, without changing the shared helper or the value.

Show O2 Engine K23,600 at his last visit and O3 Engine K21,600 now, in two adjacent columns, each with its corresponding verbatim Engine rate explanation. All displayed monetary figures are verbatim Engine outputs. No subtraction, computed difference, “higher/lower” amount, direction word, performance icon or fourth/synthesised rate. Do not invoke the existing `CurrencyInsight` comparison computation for Daniel. Its own-simulation direction/cause rules stay unchanged; they are finite comparison-surface precedent, not a general requirement to add direction to every example. **Dex's supplied note:** if precedent or the language guard requires an explicit direction word, stop and report rather than adding one.
8. **Agents:** GPT-6.1 Sol high for source activation and independent activation verification; GPT-6.1 Sol extra high (`xhigh`) for implementation, as requested by the Founder. Authoring, coordination and independent verification remain distinct roles.
9. **Standing carry-through:** §363 applies. Once source activation is effective, Dex launches the separate Implementer without another Founder prompt, respecting the copy gate and all §7a gates. §331 governed auto-merge applies without another per-merge prompt; no standing delegation is created.

### Decisions 1–5 as put and accepted, verbatim decision-sheet extract

## 1. What should Sarah’s Engine read compute?

**[Verified]** Research Sarah has local savings and future contributions, initially a USD 1,000 fee due 1 October 2027, then a fee payable in local currency. She has no declared USD holding in this story. [Stimulus, `ScenarioStimulus`][R].

**[Inferred — options]** A: read a USD fee at a fixture rate only; smallest, but omits the denomination change. B: read one declared fee denominated in USD **or** ZMW, its due date, fixture rate and explicit `asOf`; covers that change. C: add savings, contributions and funding assessment; introduces projections and D2 risk.

**[Inferred — recommendation]** B. Outputs: original declared fee/currency/deadline, canonical safe-integer local minor units and display, applicable rate basis, explanation/limits, `asOf` and `sarah-read-v1`. USD fee × rate gives a local **example fee estimate**; a ZMW fee is read directly, without FX multiplication. No USD holding, contribution forecast, coverage or posture. The Founder must lock exact fields and permitted change paths. Authority: [Pack, “Inventory seeds”/“Working definition of stable”][P]; [Contract, “Scoped semantic interface disposition”][C]; [STATUS §350/§357][S]. **Required before Sarah activation: yes.**

## 2. How does the read retain the D2 fence?

**[Verified]** Obligation-anchored progress is hypothesis-only, neither product direction nor accepted capability; prohibited participant claims include paraphrases. [STATUS §343, “No-claim fence”][S].

**[Inferred — options]** A: separate fee facts and rate assumptions, with no relationship to savings. B: show savings and fee together, even without subtraction; visual comparison can imply progress. C: percentage, gap, target meter or “on track”; unavailable under the retained fence.

**[Inferred — recommendation]** A. Proposed framing for review: “Fictional course fee. This figure uses an example rate; it is not a payment quote. This read does not establish whether Sarah can pay the fee.” No percentage, gap, progress chart, sufficiency, “closer”, gain/loss colour or paraphrased D2 claim. No “hedge/hedging”. Future Home must preserve these limits and add no financial inference. Authority: [STATUS §343/§347/§367][S]; [Contract, “Scoped semantic interface disposition”][C]. **Required before Sarah activation: yes; final Home wording separately locked later.**

## 3. Which figures, currency and rates should be used?

**[Verified]** ZMW research figures are 6,000 set aside, 2,000 monthly, 24,000 planned and 29,500 fee; five authored currency variants exist. The changed fee is a local obligation, not an FX conversion. [Fixtures, `SARAH_FIGURES`][F]; [Stimulus][R]. ZMW 27 is the only current simulated ZMW source; historical example rates are separately permitted, and research/live FX stay unchanged. [STATUS §359/§365 decision 2][S].

**[Inferred — options]** A: new Engine-owned literal goldens, one USD/ZMW pair. B: import research figures as computational fixtures; couples authored research to runtime and expands to five denominations.

**[Inferred — recommendation]** A: explicitly redeclare USD 1,000 and changed ZMW 29,500 as fictional Engine inputs; never infer a 29.5 rate from that fee. Current 27 derives from the existing Engine constant, with no second current literal. Candidate goldens: USD 1,000 at 27 → K27,000/2,700,000 ngwee; fixed ZMW 29,500 → 2,950,000 ngwee regardless of FX. Historical rates, observation dates and deadline need explicit Founder selection; future runtime fixtures must not import test goldens. Authority: [STATUS §350/§357/§359/§365][S]. **Required before Sarah activation: yes.**

## 4. Separate Sarah function or generalised example read?

**[Inferred — options]** A: separate pure `computeSarahRead`/`SarahRead`. B: generalised example computation; refactors Daniel and introduces broader schema/change control.

**[Inferred — recommendation]** A. Mirror Daniel’s explicit time, module version, BigInt rounding, safe-integer guard and fixed locale; independently define fee semantics. Avoid an `EngineState` extension, posture mapping, clock/storage dependence or generic registry refactor. Authority: [STATUS §346/§350/§351/§357/§358][S]; [Daniel, `computeDanielRead`][D]; decision bullets in [ADR 0013][A13], [0014][A14], [0015][A15] and [0027][A27]. **Required before Sarah activation: yes.**

## 5. Engine first, or Engine and Home together?

**[Verified]** §365 decision 1 explicitly separates Sarah’s Stability slice from her later Home entry. §367 introduced the URL registry; it currently contains Daniel only. Chart redesign remains deferred until after Sarah. [STATUS §365/§367/§369][S]; [Registry, `SYNTHETIC_JOURNEY_EXAMPLES`][J].

**[Inferred — options]** A: Engine-only first, later full-Home picker entry; independent scopes and gates. B: combined Engine/Home ticket; widens the recorded sequence and requires explicit amendment plus experience/copy decisions.

**[Inferred — recommendation]** A. Later Home uses “Sarah’s course fee”, URL-only selection, example-preserving reset, own-simulation fallback/isolation and the §365/§367 experience pattern; no automatic walkthrough or chart redesign. Coexistence with mock posture requires its own surface decision, not Daniel’s permission by analogy. Authority: [STATUS §365/§367][S]; [Contract, “Bounded coexistence question”][C]. **Required before Sarah activation: yes; detailed Home decisions later.**


**Bounded adoption:** Recommended B / A / A / A / A are accepted by the Founder above, with his explicit fee/deadline/rates/dates/no-progress conditions. Candidate/proposed wording is replaced only by the later verbatim copy lock below. The quoted Home recommendations are future context, never activation. §7a supplies the precise contract/goldens/tests within those accepted outputs; no savings or funding model is accepted.

    # Founder decision — D2 currency-only carve-out and currency-match open questions (verbatim)

    - **Founder:** Musalwa Hibajene (repo owner mhibajene)
    - **When:** 2026-10-13 20:03 AWST (clock checked in the same turn), in MonoCode via Dex (§9.11)
    - **Context:** Dex's pressure test of `../SARAH-CURRENCY-MATCH-V2-CONTRACT-001.md` (Founder-supplied, read at main e67c86e) raised §343 D2 compatibility and seven open questions.

    ## Verbatim
    > D2 currency-only carve-out. The Engine may state the relationship between the currency Sarah's savings are held in and the currency her fee is fixed in, and nothing else. The read takes no amounts as input and produces no coverage, gap, comparison, progress, sufficiency, ability-to-pay or action output. Its explanation never names a savings or fee figure. Wherever it is shown, Sarah's limits sentence ("This read does not establish whether Sarah can pay the fee.") stays visible next to it. Any read that uses savings amounts remains under the full §343 fence and needs its own Founder decision.Make the carve-out specific to this module and version. Name sarah-currency-match-v1, so it can't spread by analogy to future savings reads. Add a test that pins the explanation strings so they contain no digits or currency symbols. That way the amount-blind rule is enforced in the copy as well as the types.
    >
    > Other open questions as recommended

    ## Open questions resolved "as recommended" (Dex's recommendations in the preceding message)
    1. Keep `savingsCurrency: 'ZMW'` as an explicit input (E2 enforceable).
    2. Earlier state via `priorFeeFixedCurrency`, not fixture names (no story knowledge in the Engine).
    3. `asOf` optional (echoed only when supplied; never defaulted from a clock).
    4. Drop C-USD; E4 rejects prior=ZMW→current=USD as well as prior=current. Only P-USD, P-ZMW, C-ZMW remain.
    5. Keep `rateAffectsFeeLocalCost` (enables the cross-read alignment test with sarah-read-v1).
    6. Sue reconciles P-ZMW wording with the v1 kwacha sentence ("The exchange rate does not change this declared amount.").
    7. Research route only for now; no Home consumer.

    ## Corrections to the brief adopted
    - §364 RAP cadence exists and applies (source PR + separate permanent-main RAP rebind).
    - `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` changes travel in source PRs, not the runtime allowlist.
    - The research sequence (brief §9) needs its own later brief; the research route stays unreleased.

    ## Gates before activation
    Sue review of the three explanation strings → Founder copy lock → activation (sole nested Lane V; sequenced after the post-Sarah bundle Ticket A per current order unless the Founder reorders).

    **Effect:** decision input only; not an activation, §7/§7a record or copy lock.

    - **Founder:** Musalwa Hibajene (repo owner mhibajene)

    ## Locked explanation strings (character-exact, curly apostrophes)
    - **P-USD:** Sarah’s savings are in Zambian kwacha, while her course fee is fixed in US dollars. The fee’s kwacha cost depends on the example exchange rate.
    - **P-ZMW:** Sarah’s savings are in Zambian kwacha, and her course fee is fixed in the same currency. The fee’s kwacha cost does not depend on the example exchange rate.
    - **C-ZMW:** Sarah’s savings are in Zambian kwacha, and her course fee is now fixed in the same currency. The fee’s kwacha cost no longer depends on the example exchange rate.

    Wherever shown, beside: "This read does not establish whether Sarah can pay the fee." (sarah-read-v1 limits, verbatim).

**Founder record provenance:** Musalwa Hibajene (repo owner `mhibajene`), 14 Oct 2026 **16:25 AWST**, clock checked in the same turn, in MonoCode via Dex (§9.11). The following is the full word-for-word Founder direction supplied in `FOUNDER_DIRECTION_VERBATIM.md`; the attachment files remain uncommitted and are identified by title/checksum only in §387. This later direction supersedes the earlier handoff's “activate cohesion now”.

### Verbatim Founder direction

> Dex, four briefs attached: COHESION-TICKET-BRIEF-001-FINAL, ACTIVITY-VISUALISER-TICKET-BRIEF-001, STABILITY-CLAIMS-TICKET-BRIEF-001 and the cohesion handover. Keep the repo clean: don't commit any brief file, and treat the attachments as the source of truth.
>
> Queue to research release:
> 0. Governance and ops clean-up (now)
> 1. Cohesion ticket
> 2. Activity visualiser
> 3. Stability claims
> 4. Stability-done exit record
> 5. Release ticket
>
> Record items 1-3 on main as queued decisions only. Activate each one only after the one before it has closed, and re-pin to the new main each time.
>
> 0) Governance and ops clean-up, start now
> Goal: remove the friction that has slowed every ticket. Scope:
> - Add a typecheck for lib/engine/**.
> - Fix the 30 existing type errors in test files.
> - Investigate and fix the auto-merge stall and the Vercel git_info_fail.
> - Write the verifier rule into the runbook: run= is always the GitHub e2e run URL.
> Rules: tooling, CI and test types only. No user-facing changes, no Home or Engine runtime changes, and no test weakened or skipped. If a fix would touch a Home file or change behaviour, stop and raise it, because it would collide with tickets 1-3. Run it as its own ticket: activate source-first, then implementer, independent verifier on the exact head, and closeout.
>
> 1) Cohesion: COHESION-TICKET-BRIEF-001-FINAL. All 11 decisions and Sue's strings are locked. Failing tests T1-T11 first.
>
> 2) Activity visualiser: ACTIVITY-VISUALISER-TICKET-BRIEF-001. Locked: OPEN-1 (the chart text list is the only place events are itemised, Ticket B's "What changed" copy stays byte-identical, and "Since you were last here" defers to the chart list) and Sue's S1-S4 and S6. Once cohesion has merged: re-pin, re-run the collision check, fill in Ticket B's strings for V13, then failing tests V1-V13. Scope is own simulation on the synthetic journey only. PositionLine wraps the shared ExampleLineChart, with no new one-off chart.
>
> 3) Stability claims: STABILITY-CLAIMS-TICKET-BRIEF-001. All strings are locked, including the notice wording, "within the usual range" and Sue's replacements for the simulated wording. The PolicyDisclosure "may lose value" warning, the earn tile, and "Conservative yield"/"return opportunity" stay byte-identical. Failing tests SC1-SC15. Activate after the visualiser has closed.
>
> For every ticket: the brief is execution-ready, not beyond challenge. Interrogate it, challenge assumptions, and use bounded technical judgment. Surface any finding with evidence, classified by its impact on the locked product contract:
> - No contract impact (implementation detail): decide it yourself and note it in closeout.
> - Contract-adjacent (a test, file or string outside the allowlist that doesn't change what the user sees): propose it and proceed only once I confirm.
> - Contract change (the user-facing outcome, a locked string, or scope): stop and bring it to me with the evidence.
>
> Mechanics for every ticket: activate source-first (§7/§7a). Do the §364 rebind only if a RAP-mandatory source is touched. Write failing tests first, then run an independent verifier on the exact head (run= is the GitHub e2e run), then closeout with a Production check at 1440, 390 and 320@200%.
>
> Stop rules: no arrow or {from} → {to} line, no repeated figures, and no progress, sufficiency or preservation claims.
>
> The remaining open items (whether the earn tile is visible, the currency-match research brief, and the compliance check on the risk warning) stay for after release, so I've left them out.

**Activation / exact scope / checks / stops:** Live §7a above is the execution brief, including its exact eight implementation paths, named failing-first checks, 36-error baseline correction, all-lib strict-check result, evidence-qualified auto-merge recovery proposal and unresolved Vercel diagnostic. No runtime implementation occurs in this source. Finite non-lane support is the correct class: repository tooling/process only, no product/research lane occupancy, new D-number or standing support delegation. §363 separate Implementer launch and §331 exact-head independent verification govern. External settings require an exact evidence-backed proposal and stop for Dex/Founder confirmation; no such setting change is currently evidenced or authorised. No weakening, skip or Home/lib runtime repair is allowed.

**Source-first / RAP / stop:** This authority-effect source changes RAP-mandatory STATUS and AGENTS: commit sources first, regenerate deterministic branch RAP in a separate commit, independent exact-head GPT-6.1 Sol high PASS with hosted E2E `run=`, required hosted checks and governed squash auto-merge, then a separate independently verified projection-only permanent-main RAP rebind (§364), fetch main and `bridge:rap:check` PASS.
